 54+  667D 17                   RLA
 55+  667E 38 F8                JR      C, .wait_rtc
 56+  6680
 57+  6680                      ; Вычитываем BCD данные с атомарным сохранением в текстовые буферы
 58+  6680                      ; 1. Секунды (Ячейка #00)

 77+  66AD 32 43 66       LD (txt_hour+1), A

 81+  66B5 C1             POP BC

 83+  66B8
 84+  66B8              ; --- Атомарное низкоуровневое чтение байта из К512ВИ1 ---
 85+  66B8              read_byte_raw

 87+  66BB ED 79                OUT     (C), A

 90+  66C2 C9                   RET

 92+  66C3              ; --- Атомарная низкоуровневая запись байта в К512ВИ1 ---

 95+  66C6 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
 96+  66C8 01 F7 BF             LD      BC, #BFF7
 97+  66CB ED 69                OUT     (C), L
 98+  66CD C9                   RET

100+  66CE              open_cmos

102+  66D1 3E 80                LD      A, #80

104+  66D5 C9                   RET
105+  66D6
106+  66D6              close_cmos

108+  66D9 AF                   XOR     A
109+  66DA ED 79                OUT     (C), A
110+  66DC C9                   RET
111+  66DD
112+  66DD              ; --- Распаковка BCD полубайтов в ASCII символы ---
113+  66DD              split_bcd

116+  66E2 0F             RRCA
116+  66E3 0F             RRCA

122+  66EC 6F                   LD      L, A
123+  66ED C9                   RET

 11+  66EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  66EE              ; Load the separately stored NeoGS firmware file from TR-DOS.
 13+  66EE              ; neoprog.C layout: [WORD bootstrap_length][bootstrap bytes][firmware bytes].
 14+  66EE              ; OUT: Carry clear, complete package at #C000 and exact file length saved.
 15+  66EE              ; Uses the same verified TR-DOS 5.04T catalogue/sector API as Stage 5.
 16+  66EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  66EE              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  66EE
 18+  66EE                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  66EE 21 74 6A             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  66F1 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD

 22+  66F7 ED B0                ldir

 24+  66FB 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  66FE 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  6700 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  6703 0E 0A                ld      c, 10

 29+  6708 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  6709 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  670B CA C3 67             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  670E
 33+  670E                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  670E F5                   push    af
 35+  670F AF                   xor     a
 36+  6710 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  6713 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  6716 F1                   pop     af
 39+  6717 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6719 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  671C 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  671F 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  6720 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  6723 B0                   or      b
 45+  6724 C2 C7 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  6727
 47+  6727                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  6727 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  672A 22 82 6A             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  672D 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  672E B5                   or      l
 52+  672F CA CB 67             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  6732 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  6733 B7                   or      a
 55+  6734 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  6736 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  6737 FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6739 DA CB 67             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  673C              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  673C                      ; #C000 has at most 16 KiB available before address wrap at #0000.
 61+  673C 7C                   ld      a, h
 62+  673D FE 40                cp      #40
 63+  673F 38 08                jr      c, .package_size_ok
 64+  6741 C2 CB 67             jp      nz, .bad_length
 65+  6744 7D                   ld      a, l
 66+  6745 B7                   or      a
 67+  6746 C2 CB 67             jp      nz, .bad_length
 68+  6749              .package_size_ok:
 69+  6749                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 70+  6749 2A 82 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 71+  674C 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 72+  674F 19                   add     hl, de
 73+  6750 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 74+  6753 BC                   cp      h
 75+  6754 C2 C7 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 76+  6757 32 7E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 77+  675A
 78+  675A 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 79+  675D FE 10                cp      16
 80+  675F D2 C7 67             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 81+  6762 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 82+  6765 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 83+  6768 32 7F 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 84+  676B 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 85+  676E 22 7C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 86+  6771
 87+  6771              .read_sector_loop:
 88+  6771 3A 7F 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
 89+  6774 57                   ld      d, a
 90+  6775 3A 80 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
 91+  6778 5F                   ld      e, a
 92+  6779 2A 7C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_load_buffer)
 93+  677C 06 01                ld      b, 1
 94+  677E 0E 05                ld      c, 5
 95+  6780 AF                   xor     a
 96+  6781 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 97+  6784 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 98+  6787 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 99+  678A 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
100+  678D 47                   ld      b, a
101+  678E 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
102+  6791 B0                   or      b
103+  6792 C2 CF 67             jp      nz, .sector_read_failed
104+  6795
105+  6795                      ; Next 256-byte sector goes to the next host buffer page.
106+  6795 2A 7C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_load_buffer)
107+  6798 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
108+  679B 19                   add     hl, de
109+  679C 22 7C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
110+  679F
111+  679F                      ; TR-DOS internal sectors are 0..15; then advance the track.
112+  679F 3A 80 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
113+  67A2 3C                   inc     a
114+  67A3 FE 10                cp      16
115+  67A5 38 0D                jr      c, .same_track
116+  67A7 AF                   xor     a
117+  67A8 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
118+  67AB 3A 7F 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
119+  67AE 3C                   inc     a

121+  67B2 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
122+  67B4              .same_track:
123+  67B4 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
124+  67B7              .sector_advanced:
125+  67B7 3A 7E 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
126+  67BA 3D                   dec     a
127+  67BB 32 7E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
128+  67BE C2 71 67             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
129+  67C1
130+  67C1 AF                   xor     a
131+  67C2 C9                   ret
132+  67C3
133+  67C3              .file_not_found:
134+  67C3 3E 01                ld      a, 1
135+  67C5 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
136+  67C7              .bad_descriptor:

138+  67C9 18 06                jr      .load_failed
139+  67CB              .bad_length:

142+  67CF              .sector_read_failed:

146+  67D4 37                   scf

148+  67D6
149+  67D6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
150+  67D6              ; upload_firmware
151+  67D6              ; IN : HL = neoprog package in host RAM, DE = total package length.
152+  67D6              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
153+  67D6              ;
154+  67D6              ; Cold/warm reset path:
155+  67D6              ;   #F4 cold restart
156+  67D6              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
157+  67D6              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
158+  67D6              ;   #13 jump to $4000
159+  67D6              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
160+  67D6              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
161+  67D6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
162+  67D6              upload_firmware:
162+  67D6
163+  67D6                      ; HL points to the package read once from TR-DOS; DE is its total length.
164+  67D6                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
165+  67D6 22 84 6A             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
166+  67D9 ED 53 86 6A          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
167+  67DD
168+  67DD                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
169+  67DD 7A                   ld      a, d
170+  67DE B7                   or      a
171+  67DF 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
172+  67E1 7B                   ld      a, e
173+  67E2 FE 03                cp      3
174+  67E4 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
175+  67E7              .package_header_present:
176+  67E7 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
177+  67E8 32 8A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
178+  67EB 23                   inc     hl
179+  67EC 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
180+  67ED 32 8B 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
181+  67F0 2A 8A 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
182+  67F3 7C                   ld      a, h
183+  67F4 B5                   or      l
184+  67F5 CA 52 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
185+  67F8 7C                   ld      a, h
186+  67F9 FE 40                cp      #40
187+  67FB D2 52 69             jp      nc, .upload_failed
188+  67FE
189+  67FE                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
190+  67FE 2A 86 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
191+  6801 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
192+  6804 B7                   or      a
193+  6805 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
194+  6807 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
195+  680A ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
196+  680E B7                   or      a
197+  680F ED 52                sbc     hl, de
198+  6811 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
199+  6814 22 8E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
200+  6817
201+  6817                      ; Resolve both section pointers inside the same package buffer.
202+  6817 2A 84 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
203+  681A 23                   inc     hl
204+  681B 23                   inc     hl
205+  681C 22 88 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
206+  681F ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
207+  6823 19                   add     hl, de
208+  6824 22 8C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
209+  6827
210+  6827                      ; Firmware image must fit below $3400, preserving at least 3 KiB for the stack below SP=$3FFF.
211+  6827 2A 8E 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
212+  682A 7C                   ld      a, h
213+  682B B7                   or      a
214+  682C C2 36 68             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
215+  682F 7D                   ld      a, l
216+  6830 B7                   or      a
217+  6831 CA 52 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
218+  6834 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
219+  6836              .firmware_high_nonzero:
220+  6836 7C                   ld      a, h
221+  6837 FE 34                cp      #34
222+  6839 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
223+  683B C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
224+  683E 7D                   ld      a, l
225+  683F B7                   or      a
226+  6840 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
227+  6843              .firmware_length_valid:
228+  6843 AF                   xor     a
229+  6844 32 9A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
230+  6847 32 9B 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
231+  684A 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
232+  684D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
233+  684F 32 9C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
234+  6852 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
235+  6855
236+  6855                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
237+  6855                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
238+  6855 3E 01                ld      a, 1
239+  6857 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
240+  685A 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
241+  685C CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
242+  685F DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
243+  6862
244+  6862                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
245+  6862                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
246+  6862                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
247+  6862                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
248+  6862 3E 02                ld      a, 2
249+  6864 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
250+  6867 CD 54 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
251+  686A 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
252+  686C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
253+  686E 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
254+  6871 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
255+  6873 CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
256+  6876 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
257+  6879 3E 02                ld      a, 2
258+  687B 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
259+  687E CD 54 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
260+  6881 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
261+  6884              .stock_rom_ready:
262+  6884
263+  6884                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
264+  6884                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
265+  6884                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
266+  6884                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
267+  6884 3E 03                ld      a, 3
268+  6886 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
269+  6889 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
270+  688C ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
271+  6890 B7                   or      a
272+  6891 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
273+  6893 22 92 6A             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
274+  6896 7D                   ld      a, l
275+  6897 CD C4 69             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
276+  689A DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
277+  689D 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
278+  689F CD A7 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
279+  68A2 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
280+  68A5
281+  68A5                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
282+  68A5 3A 93 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
283+  68A8 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
284+  68AB DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
285+  68AE AF                   xor     a
286+  68AF CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
287+  68B2 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
288+  68B5 3E 40                ld      a, #40
289+  68B7 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
290+  68BA DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
291+  68BD
292+  68BD                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
293+  68BD 3E 04                ld      a, 4
294+  68BF 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
295+  68C2 2A 88 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
296+  68C5 ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
297+  68C9              .bootstrap_block_loop:
298+  68C9 7A                   ld      a, d
299+  68CA B3                   or      e
300+  68CB 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
301+  68CD 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
302+  68CE CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
303+  68D1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
304+  68D4 23                   inc     hl
305+  68D5 1B                   dec     de
306+  68D6 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
307+  68D8              .bootstrap_block_done:
308+  68D8
309+  68D8                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
310+  68D8                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
311+  68D8 3E 05                ld      a, 5
312+  68DA 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
313+  68DD AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
314+  68DE CD C4 69             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
315+  68E1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
316+  68E4 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
317+  68E6 CD A7 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
318+  68E9 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
319+  68EC 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
320+  68EE CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
321+  68F1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
322+  68F4
323+  68F4                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
324+  68F4 3E 06                ld      a, 6
325+  68F6 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
326+  68F9 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
327+  68FC DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
328+  68FF FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
329+  6901 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
330+  6904
331+  6904                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
332+  6904 3E 07                ld      a, 7
333+  6906 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
334+  6909 2A 8E 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
335+  690C 7D                   ld      a, l
336+  690D CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
337+  6910 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
338+  6913 7C                   ld      a, h
339+  6914 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
340+  6917 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
341+  691A CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
342+  691D DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
343+  6920 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
344+  6922 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
345+  6925
346+  6925                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
347+  6925 3E 08                ld      a, 8
348+  6927 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
349+  692A 2A 8C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
350+  692D ED 5B 8E 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
351+  6931              .firmware_stream_loop:
352+  6931 7A                   ld      a, d
353+  6932 B3                   or      e
354+  6933 28 0B                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
355+  6935 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
356+  6936 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
357+  6939 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
358+  693C 23                   inc     hl
359+  693D 1B                   dec     de
360+  693E 18 F1                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
361+  6940
362+  6940              .firmware_stream_done:
363+  6940                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
364+  6940 3E 09                ld      a, 9
365+  6942 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
366+  6945 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
367+  6948 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
368+  694B FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
369+  694D C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
370+  6950
371+  6950 AF                   xor     a
372+  6951 C9                   ret
373+  6952
374+  6952              .upload_failed:
375+  6952 37                   scf
376+  6953 C9                   ret
377+  6954
378+  6954              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
379+  6954              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
380+  6954              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
381+  6954              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
382+  6954
383+  6954              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
384+  6954              gs_boot_probe_rom:
385+  6954 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
386+  6956 CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
387+  6959 D8                   ret     c
388+  695A CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
389+  695D D8                   ret     c
390+  695E 32 94 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
391+  6961 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
392+  6964 D8                   ret     c
393+  6965 32 95 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
394+  6968 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
395+  696B D8                   ret     c
396+  696C 32 96 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
397+  696F 3A 94 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_low)
398+  6972 47                   ld      b, a
399+  6973 3A 95 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid)
400+  6976 B0                   or      b
401+  6977 47                   ld      b, a
402+  6978 3A 96 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
403+  697B B0                   or      b
404+  697C 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
405+  697E 37                   scf
406+  697F C9                   ret
407+  6980              .rom_probe_ok:
408+  6980 B7                   or      a
409+  6981 C9                   ret
410+  6982
411+  6982              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
412+  6982              gs_boot_send_command_long:
413+  6982 32 97 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
414+  6985 3A 97 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
415+  6988 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
416+  698A D5                   push    de
417+  698B 16 10                ld      d, 16
418+  698D              .long_outer:
419+  698D 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
420+  6990              .long_inner:
421+  6990 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
422+  6992 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
423+  6995 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
424+  6997 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
425+  6999 0B                   dec     bc
426+  699A 78                   ld      a, b
427+  699B B1                   or      c
428+  699C 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
429+  699E 15                   dec     d
430+  699F 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
431+  69A1 D1                   pop     de
432+  69A2 37                   scf
433+  69A3 C9                   ret
434+  69A4              .long_done:
435+  69A4 D1                   pop     de
436+  69A5 B7                   or      a
437+  69A6 C9                   ret
438+  69A7
439+  69A7              gs_boot_send_command:
440+  69A7 32 97 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
441+  69AA 3A 97 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
442+  69AD D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
443+  69AF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
444+  69B2              .command_wait:
445+  69B2 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
446+  69B4 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
447+  69B7 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
448+  69B9 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
449+  69BB 0B                   dec     bc
450+  69BC 78                   ld      a, b
451+  69BD B1                   or      c
452+  69BE 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
453+  69C0 37                   scf
454+  69C1 C9                   ret
455+  69C2              .command_done:
456+  69C2 B7                   or      a
457+  69C3 C9                   ret
458+  69C4
459+  69C4              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
460+  69C4              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
461+  69C4              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
462+  69C4 32 98 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
463+  69C7 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
464+  69CA              .argument_wait_free:
465+  69CA DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
466+  69CC 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
467+  69CF CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
468+  69D1 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
469+  69D3 0B                   dec     bc
470+  69D4 78                   ld      a, b
471+  69D5 B1                   or      c
472+  69D6 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
473+  69D8 37                   scf
474+  69D9 C9                   ret
475+  69DA              .argument_slot_free:
476+  69DA 3A 98 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
477+  69DD D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
478+  69DF B7                   or      a
479+  69E0 C9                   ret
480+  69E1
481+  69E1              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
482+  69E1 32 98 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
483+  69E4 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
484+  69E7              .data_wait_free:
485+  69E7 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
486+  69E9 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
487+  69EC CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
488+  69EE 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
489+  69F0 0B                   dec     bc
490+  69F1 78                   ld      a, b
491+  69F2 B1                   or      c
492+  69F3 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
493+  69F5 37                   scf
494+  69F6 C9                   ret
495+  69F7              .data_slot_free:
496+  69F7 3A 98 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
497+  69FA D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
498+  69FC 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
499+  69FF              .data_wait_consumed:
500+  69FF DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
501+  6A01 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
502+  6A04 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
503+  6A06 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
504+  6A08 0B                   dec     bc
505+  6A09 78                   ld      a, b
506+  6A0A B1                   or      c
507+  6A0B 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
508+  6A0D 37                   scf
509+  6A0E C9                   ret
510+  6A0F              .data_consumed:
511+  6A0F E5                   push    hl
512+  6A10 2A 9A 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
513+  6A13 23                   inc     hl
514+  6A14 22 9A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
515+  6A17 E1                   pop     hl
516+  6A18 B7                   or      a
517+  6A19 C9                   ret
518+  6A1A
519+  6A1A              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
520+  6A1A 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
521+  6A1D              .receive_wait:
522+  6A1D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
523+  6A1F 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
524+  6A22 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
525+  6A24 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
526+  6A26 0B                   dec     bc
527+  6A27 78                   ld      a, b
528+  6A28 B1                   or      c
529+  6A29 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
530+  6A2B 37                   scf
531+  6A2C C9                   ret
532+  6A2D              .receive_ready:
533+  6A2D DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
534+  6A2F 32 99 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
535+  6A32 B7                   or      a
536+  6A33 C9                   ret
537+  6A34
538+  6A34              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
539+  6A34              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
540+  6A34              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
541+  6A34              neogs_wait_command_done:
541+  6A34
542+  6A34 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
543+  6A37              .cmd_wait:
544+  6A37 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
545+  6A39 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
546+  6A3B 28 07                jr      z, .cmd_done
547+  6A3D 0B                   dec     bc
548+  6A3E 78                   ld      a, b
549+  6A3F B1                   or      c
550+  6A40 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_wait
551+  6A42 37                   scf
552+  6A43 C9                   ret
553+  6A44              .cmd_done:
554+  6A44 B7                   or      a
555+  6A45 C9                   ret
556+  6A46
557+  6A46              neogs_wait_command_done_long:
557+  6A46
558+  6A46 D5                   push    de
559+  6A47 16 10                ld      d, #10
560+  6A49              .cmd_long_outer:
561+  6A49 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
562+  6A4C              .cmd_long_inner:
563+  6A4C DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
564+  6A4E CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
565+  6A50 28 0B                jr      z, .cmd_long_done
566+  6A52 0B                   dec     bc
567+  6A53 78                   ld      a, b
568+  6A54 B1                   or      c
569+  6A55 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_long_inner
570+  6A57 15                   dec     d
571+  6A58 20 EF                jr      nz, .cmd_long_outer
572+  6A5A D1                   pop     de
573+  6A5B 37                   scf
574+  6A5C C9                   ret
575+  6A5D              .cmd_long_done:
576+  6A5D D1                   pop     de
577+  6A5E B7                   or      a
578+  6A5F C9                   ret
579+  6A60
580+  6A60              neogs_wait_data_byte:
580+  6A60
581+  6A60 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT
582+  6A63              .data_wait:
583+  6A63 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
584+  6A65 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
585+  6A67 20 07                jr      nz, .data_ready
586+  6A69 0B                   dec     bc
587+  6A6A 78                   ld      a, b
588+  6A6B B1                   or      c
589+  6A6C 20 F5                jr      nz, .data_wait
590+  6A6E 37                   scf
591+  6A6F C9                   ret
592+  6A70              .data_ready:
593+  6A70 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
594+  6A72 B7                   or      a
595+  6A73 C9                   ret
596+  6A74
597+  6A74              ; TR-DOS NeoGS package loader state and fixed 8-character filename.
598+  6A74 6E 65 6F 70  neo_fw_filename:        db "neoprog "

# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6A9F              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6A9F              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6A9F              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6A9F              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6A9F              ;
  6+  6A9F              ; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
  7+  6A9F              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6A9F              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
  9+  6A9F              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6A9F              ;
 11+  6A9F              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 12+  6A9F              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 13+  6A9F              ; =========================================================================
 14+  6A9F
 15+  6A9F              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 16+  6A9F
 17+  6A9F              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 18+  6A9F              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 19+  6A9F
 20+  6A9F              EXECUTE_X86_FILE_STAGE:
 20+  6A9F
 21+  6A9F 22 FB 78             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME), hl
 22+  6AA2 32 FD 78             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE), a
 23+  6AA5 ED 73 F3 78          ld      (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP), sp
 24+  6AA9 31 FF BF             ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it
 25+  6AAC
 26+  6AAC                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated
 26+  6AAC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
 26+  6AAC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
 26+  6AAD 21 0D 79    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated
 26+  6AB0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
 26+  6AB3 E1          >                    POP     HL
 26+  6AB4             >                ENDIF
 27+  6AB4
 28+  6AB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
 29+  6AB4                      ; Stage 5.0: deterministic diagnostic before accessing TR-DOS.
 30+  6AB4                      ; B8 AA BB 40 90 F4 = MOV AX,BBAA / INC AX / NOP / HLT.
 31+  6AB4                      ; Expected result: status=0, AX=BBAB.
 32+  6AB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
 33+  6AB4                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_start
 33+  6AB4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
 33+  6AB4 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
 33+  6AB5 21 43 79    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_start
 33+  6AB8 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
 33+  6ABB E1          >                    POP     HL
 33+  6ABC             >                ENDIF
 34+  6ABC
 35+  6ABC                      ; Exercise unknown-command release and explicit firmware reset before DMA.
 36+  6ABC 3E FE                ld      a, #FE
 37+  6ABE D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
 38+  6AC0 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
 39+  6AC3 DA EC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 40+  6AC6
 41+  6AC6 3E 01                ld      a, CMD_RESET
 42+  6AC8 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
 43+  6ACA CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
 44+  6ACD DA EC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 45+  6AD0
 46+  6AD0 21 B9 78             ld      hl, x86_diag_program
 47+  6AD3 01 06 00             ld      bc, x86_diag_program_end - x86_diag_program
 48+  6AD6 CD 22 70             call    neogs_send_dma_block
 49+  6AD9 DA E0 6D             jp      c, .diag_dma_error
 50+  6ADC
 51+  6ADC CD 7E 70             call    neogs_run_and_get_result
 52+  6ADF DA EC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 53+  6AE2
 54+  6AE2 3A 06 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
 55+  6AE5 B7                   or      a
 56+  6AE6 C2 F8 6D             jp      nz, .diag_execution_error
 57+  6AE9 2A 07 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
 58+  6AEC 11 AB BB             ld      de, #BBAB
 59+  6AEF B7                   or      a
 60+  6AF0 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
 61+  6AF2 C2 87 6F             jp      nz, .diag_wrong_result

209+  6C92                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
210+  6C92                      ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
211+  6C92                      ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
212+  6C92                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
213+  6C92 2A FB 78             ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
214+  6C95 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
215+  6C98 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
216+  6C9B ED B0                ldir
217+  6C9D 3A FD 78             ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
218+  6CA0 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
219+  6CA3 3E 09                ld      a, 9
220+  6CA5 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
221+  6CA8 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
222+  6CAA CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
223+  6CAD 79                   ld      a, c
224+  6CAE FE FF                cp      #FF
225+  6CB0 CA A8 6F             jp      z, .file_not_found
226+  6CB3
227+  6CB3                      ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
228+  6CB3                      ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
229+  6CB3 79                   ld      a, c
230+  6CB4 F5                   push    af
231+  6CB5 AF                   xor     a
232+  6CB6 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
233+  6CB9 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
234+  6CBC F1                   pop     af
235+  6CBD 0E 08                ld      c, 8
236+  6CBF CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
237+  6CC2 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
238+  6CC5 47                   ld      b, a
239+  6CC6 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
240+  6CC9 B0                   or      b
241+  6CCA C2 C0 6F             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
242+  6CCD
243+  6CCD                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok
243+  6CCD             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
243+  6CCD E5          >                    PUSH    HL
243+  6CCE 21 3E 7B    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
243+  6CD1 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
243+  6CD4 E1          >                    POP     HL
243+  6CD5             >                ENDIF
244+  6CD5
245+  6CD5                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
246+  6CD5                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
247+  6CD5                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
248+  6CD5                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
249+  6CD5                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
250+  6CD5                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
251+  6CD5                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
252+  6CD5 ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
253+  6CD9 78                   ld      a, b
254+  6CDA B1                   or      c
255+  6CDB CA B4 6F             jp      z, .empty_file_error
256+  6CDE ED 43 FE 78          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
257+  6CE2
258+  6CE2                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
259+  6CE2 78                   ld      a, b
260+  6CE3 FE 2F                cp      #2F
261+  6CE5 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
262+  6CE7 C2 ED 6F             jp      nz, .task_too_large
263+  6CEA 79                   ld      a, c
264+  6CEB FE FF                cp      #FF
265+  6CED D2 ED 6F             jp      nc, .task_too_large
266+  6CF0              .file_length_ok:
267+  6CF0                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
268+  6CF0 2A FE 78             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
269+  6CF3 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
270+  6CF6 19                   add     hl, de
271+  6CF7 5C                   ld      e, h
272+  6CF8 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
273+  6CFB B7                   or      a
274+  6CFC CA B4 6F             jp      z, .empty_file_error
275+  6CFF BB                   cp      e
276+  6D00 C2 C0 6F             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
277+  6D03 32 02 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
278+  6D06
279+  6D06 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
280+  6D09 FE 10                cp      16
281+  6D0B D2 C0 6F             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
282+  6D0E 32 04 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
283+  6D11 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
284+  6D14 32 03 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
285+  6D17 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
286+  6D1A 22 00 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
287+  6D1D
288+  6D1D              .read_sector_loop:
289+  6D1D                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
290+  6D1D 3A 03 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
291+  6D20 57                   ld      d, a
292+  6D21 3A 04 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)

302+  6D36 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)

543+  701D              .loader_restore_stack:
544+  701D 2A F3 78             ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
545+  7020 F9                   ld      sp, hl
546+  7021 C9                   ret
547+  7022
548+  7022              ; =========================================================================
549+  7022              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
550+  7022              ; =========================================================================
551+  7022
552+  7022              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
553+  7022              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
554+  7022              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
555+  7022              ;
556+  7022              ; Protocol:
557+  7022              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
558+  7022              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
559+  7022              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
560+  7022              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
561+  7022              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
562+  7022              neogs_send_dma_block:
563+  7022 78                   ld      a, b
564+  7023 B1                   or      c
565+  7024 CA 7C 70             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
566+  7027 78                   ld      a, b
567+  7028 FE 40                cp      #40
568+  702A 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
569+  702C C2 7C 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
570+  702F 79                   ld      a, c
571+  7030 B7                   or      a
572+  7031 C2 7C 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
573+  7034              .dma_length_ok:
574+  7034 22 F5 78             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
575+  7037 ED 43 F7 78          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
576+  703B
577+  703B                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
578+  703B C5                   push    bc
579+  703C E1                   pop     hl
580+  703D 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
581+  7040 19                   add     hl, de
582+  7041 7C                   ld      a, h
583+  7042 FE 30                cp      #30
584+  7044 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
585+  7046 C2 7C 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
586+  7049 7D                   ld      a, l
587+  704A B7                   or      a
588+  704B C2 7C 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
589+  704E              .dma_total_length_ok:
590+  704E 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
591+  7050 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
592+  7052 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
593+  7055 D8                   ret     c
594+  7056
595+  7056                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
596+  7056                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
597+  7056                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
598+  7056                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
599+  7056                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
600+  7056 F3                   di
601+  7057 2A F7 78             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
602+  705A 22 F9 78             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
603+  705D 21 F9 78             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
604+  7060 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
605+  7063 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
606+  7066 ED B0                ldir
607+  7068
608+  7068 2A F5 78             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
609+  706B 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
610+  706E ED 4B F7 78          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
611+  7072 ED B0                ldir
612+  7074
613+  7074                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
614+  7074 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
615+  7076 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
616+  7078 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
617+  707B C9                   ret
618+  707C
619+  707C              .dma_bad_length:
620+  707C 37                   scf
621+  707D C9                   ret
622+  707E
623+  707E              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
624+  707E              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
625+  707E              neogs_run_and_get_result:
626+  707E 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
627+  7080 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
628+  7082 CD 9F 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
629+  7085 D8                   ret     c
630+  7086 C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
631+  7089
632+  7089              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
633+  7089              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
634+  7089 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
635+  708B D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
636+  708D CD 9F 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
637+  7090 D8                   ret     c
638+  7091 C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
639+  7094
640+  7094              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
641+  7094              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
642+  7094              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
643+  7094 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
644+  7096 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
645+  7098 CD 9F 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
646+  709B D8                   ret     c
647+  709C C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
648+  709F
649+  709F              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
650+  709F              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
651+  709F              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
652+  709F              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
653+  709F D5                   push    de
654+  70A0 16 10                ld      d, #10
655+  70A2              .run_outer:
656+  70A2 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
657+  70A5              .run_inner:
658+  70A5 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
659+  70A7 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
660+  70A9 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
661+  70AB DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
662+  70AD B7                   or      a
663+  70AE 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
664+  70B0 F5                   push    af
665+  70B1 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
666+  70B4 ED 79                out     (c), a
667+  70B6 F1                   pop     af
668+  70B7 CD 76 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
669+  70BA 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
670+  70BC
671+  70BC              .stream_control:
672+  70BC CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
673+  70BF 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
674+  70C1 B7                   or      a
675+  70C2 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
676+  70C4 FE 01                cp      1
677+  70C6 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
678+  70C8 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
679+  70CB 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
680+  70CD 32 0C 79             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
681+  70D0 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
682+  70D3 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
683+  70D5 4F                   ld      c, a
684+  70D6 3A 0C 79             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
685+  70D9 CD 68 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
686+  70DC 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
687+  70DE
688+  70DE              .stream_clear:
689+  70DE CD 1D 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
690+  70E1 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
691+  70E3
692+  70E3              .no_stream_byte:
693+  70E3 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
694+  70E5 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
695+  70E7 2B                   dec     hl
696+  70E8 7C                   ld      a, h
697+  70E9 B5                   or      l
698+  70EA 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
699+  70EC 15                   dec     d
700+  70ED 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
701+  70EF              .run_timeout:
702+  70EF D1                   pop     de
703+  70F0 37                   scf
704+  70F1 C9                   ret
705+  70F2              .run_done:
706+  70F2 D1                   pop     de
707+  70F3 B7                   or      a
708+  70F4 C9                   ret
709+  70F5
710+  70F5              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
711+  70F5              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
712+  70F5 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
713+  70F7 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
714+  70F9 CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
715+  70FC D8                   ret     c
716+  70FD C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
717+  7100
718+  7100              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
719+  7100              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
720+  7100 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
721+  7102 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
722+  7104 CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
723+  7107 D8                   ret     c
724+  7108 C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
725+  710B
726+  710B              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
727+  710B              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
728+  710B 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
729+  710D D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
730+  710F                      ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
731+  710F                      ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
732+  710F CD 9F 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
733+  7112 D8                   ret     c
734+  7113 C3 1E 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
735+  7116
736+  7116              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
737+  7116              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
738+  7116 3E 05                ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
739+  7118 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
740+  711A CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
741+  711D D8                   ret     c
742+  711E
743+  711E              neogs_get_result_frame:
744+  711E 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
745+  7120 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
746+  7122
747+  7122 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
748+  7125 D8                   ret     c
749+  7126 32 05 79             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a

830+  78CA              ; interpreter status and AX. IN: HL=payload, BC=length, A=expected status,
831+  78CA              ; DE=expected AX. OUT: carry clear on exact match, set on any transport/result
832+  78CA              ; mismatch. This deliberately accepts the expected error statuses above.
833+  78CA              neogs_run_diag_case:
834+  78CA 32 09 79             ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS), a
835+  78CD ED 53 0A 79          ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX), de
836+  78D1 CD 22 70             call    neogs_send_dma_block
837+  78D4 D8                   ret     c
838+  78D5 CD 7E 70             call    neogs_run_and_get_result
839+  78D8 D8                   ret     c
840+  78D9 3A 06 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
841+  78DC 47                   ld      b, a
842+  78DD 3A 09 79             ld      a, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS)
843+  78E0 B8                   cp      b
844+  78E1 20 0E                jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
845+  78E3 2A 07 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
846+  78E6 ED 5B 0A 79          ld      de, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX)
847+  78EA B7                   or      a
848+  78EB ED 52                sbc     hl, de
849+  78ED 20 02                jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
850+  78EF B7                   or      a
851+  78F0 C9                   ret
852+  78F1              .diag_case_failed:
853+  78F1 37                   scf
854+  78F2 C9                   ret
855+  78F3
856+  78F3              ; Data/state
857+  78F3 00 00        P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP: dw 0

  1+  8700              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8700              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
  3+  8700              ; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
  4+  8700              ; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
  5+  8700              ; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.
  6+  8700              ; =========================================================================

 82+  8775 C9                   ret

 84+  8776              ; IN: A=one output byte from the NeoGS stream.
 85+  8776              ; Printable bytes are drawn as packed 6-pixel glyphs; CR/LF/BS are handled.
 86+  8776              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar:

 88+  8777 C5                   push    bc

118+  87B1
119+  87B1                      ; Eight scanlines share the same character-cell X origin.
120+  87B1                      ; Reset the pixel coordinate for every glyph scanline.
121+  87B1 AF                   xor     a

126+  87B5                      ; glyph_pixel advances bios_dbg_xpixel by six pixels, so carrying
127+  87B5                      ; that value into the next scanline causes diagonal glyph rendering.
128+  87B5 3A B9 88             ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
129+  87B8 87                   add     a, a

131+  87BA 87                   add     a, a

169+  87F9              .mask_loop:

  1+  8967              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8967              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8967              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8967              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8967              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
  6+  8967              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8967
  8+  8967              ; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
  9+  8967              ; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
 10+  8967              ;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
 11+  8967              GetSelectedFileDescriptor:
 11+  8967
 12+  8967 3A F0 7E             ld      a, (CursorY)
 13+  896A FE 02                cp      2
 14+  896C 38 1C                jr      c, .bad_row
 15+  896E FE 0B                cp      11
 16+  8970 30 18                jr      nc, .bad_row

 18+  8974 6F                   ld      l, a
 19+  8975 26 00                ld      h, 0
 20+  8977 29                   add     hl, hl
 21+  8978 29                   add     hl, hl
 22+  8979 29                   add     hl, hl
 23+  897A 29                   add     hl, hl
 24+  897B 11 00 C0             ld      de, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
 25+  897E 19                   add     hl, de
 26+  897F
 27+  897F                      ; Reject an empty/deleted/invalid catalogue slot.
 28+  897F 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 29+  8980 FE 20                cp      32
 30+  8982 38 06                jr      c, .bad_row
 31+  8984 FE 7F                cp      127
 32+  8986 30 02                jr      nc, .bad_row
 33+  8988 B7                   or      a
 34+  8989 C9                   ret

 47+  8998 5B 4C 41 55          defb "[LAUNCH] Invoking system CODE loader for selected workspace node...", #0D, #0A, 0

 57+  89E2 AF                   xor     a
 58+  89E3 32 19 5D             ld      (#5D19), a
 59+  89E6 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a

201+  8AD3 D2 F5 8A             jp      nc, .directory_done
202+  8AD6
203+  8AD6                      ; Advance to next 16-byte descriptor. Restore saved slot counter.
204+  8AD6 E1                   pop     hl
205+  8AD7 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
206+  8ADA 19                   add     hl, de
207+  8ADB C1                   pop     bc
208+  8ADC 05                   dec     b
209+  8ADD C2 0E 8A             jp      nz, .loop_slots
210+  8AE0 C3 F7 8A             jp      .directory_success
211+  8AE3
212+  8AE3              .invalid_slot:
213+  8AE3                      ; Drop the per-slot saves, advance HL, and decrement the ORIGINAL
214+  8AE3                      ; 128-entry counter. Do not load 16 into BC (that destroys B), and
215+  8AE3                      ; do not push the per-slot saves again before re-entering the loop.
216+  8AE3 E1                   pop     hl
217+  8AE4 C1                   pop     bc
218+  8AE5 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
219+  8AE8 19                   add     hl, de
220+  8AE9 05                   dec     b
221+  8AEA C2 0E 8A             jp      nz, .loop_slots
222+  8AED C3 F7 8A             jp      .directory_success
223+  8AF0
224+  8AF0              .empty_slot:
225+  8AF0                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
226+  8AF0 E1                   pop     hl
227+  8AF1 C1                   pop     bc
228+  8AF2 C3 F7 8A             jp      .directory_success
229+  8AF5
230+  8AF5              .directory_done:
231+  8AF5                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
232+  8AF5 E1                   pop     hl
233+  8AF6 C1                   pop     bc
234+  8AF7              .directory_success:
235+  8AF7 E1                   pop     hl

 85+  E177 32 33 39 0E          DB      "239", $0E, $00, $00, $EF, $00, $00 ; Бинарный маркер числа 239
 85+  E17B 00 00 EF 00
 85+  E17F 00

 89+  E189 F9                   DB      RANDOMIZE               ; RANDOMIZE
